# Derived, not verbatim. Three gates were applied to 36,960 source pairs:
#   1. the misspelling must not itself be a real word (rejected 2,057:
#      about, accept, absorb, acceptable ...)
#   2. the correction must exist in the bundled dictionary (rejected 464)
#   3. the correction must resemble the misspelling, similarity >= 0.65
#      (rejected 5,773 junk pairs such as aacock -> helicopter at 0.25)
# Also removed: 1,826 ambiguous, 1,325 non-alphabetic, 674 too short,
# 150 already handled by context-aware rules in polish_spelling.py.
